```
218	  texFromImage(key: string, img: TexImageSource, repeatX = false, noMip = false): TexEntry | null {
219	    if (this.unavailable || !this.gl) return null;
220	    const hit = this.texs.get(key);
221	    if (hit) { hit.stamp = ++this.stamp; return hit; }
222	    const gl = this.gl;
223	    const w = (img as { width: number }).width;
224	    const h = (img as { height: number }).height;
225	    if (!w || !h) return null;
226	    const tex = gl.createTexture()!;
227	    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, tex);
228	    // ★预乘上传:mip 层平均的是预乘像素(能量正确)——直 Alpha 会让 mip 把
229	    // 透明像素的 RGB(黑)混进边缘 = 软边缘发暗(对拍实锤树冠边缘差异)。
230	    // shader 侧公式无需变:out.rgb = premul×tint×uAlpha 恰等于 straight×tint×a
231	    gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_PREMULTIPLY_ALPHA_WEBGL, true);
232	    gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL, false);
233	    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, repeatX ? gl.REPEAT : gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
234	    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
235	    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, gl.LINEAR);
236	    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, gl.LINEAR);
237	    gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, gl.RGBA, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, img);
238	    const mipped = !noMip;
239	    if (mipped) gl.generateMipmap(gl.TEXTURE_2D);   // LINEAR 档三线采样(LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR)
240	    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, null);
241	    const base = w * h * 4;
242	    const entry: TexEntry = { tex, w, h, stamp: ++this.stamp, bytes: mipped ? base * 4 / 3 : base, mipped };
243	    this.texs.set(key, entry);
244	    this.bytes += entry.bytes;
245	    this.evictLRU(key);
246	    return entry;
247	  }
248	
249	  /** 子区增量上传(小地图 dirtyChunks;画布/位图源,纹理须已存在)。
250	   *  ★WebGL2 的 DOM 源重载只有 8 参(无宽高)——曾传 9 参,Chrome 把画布
251	   *  【左上角 w×h】贴进目标区 = 脏块全被天空色污染(地图逐渐出现 #362CFF
252	   *  退化块的根因,2026-08-18 用户实报+探针定罪)。DOM 源取子区必须先抠到
253	   *  scratch 画布再整幅上传 */
254	  private scratch: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
255	  texSubUpdate(key: string, src: TexImageSource, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): void {
256	    if (this.unavailable || !this.gl) return;
257	    const e = this.texs.get(key);
258	    if (!e) return;
259	    const gl = this.gl;
260	    if (!this.scratch) this.scratch = document.createElement('canvas');
261	    if (this.scratch.width !== w || this.scratch.height !== h) {
262	      this.scratch.width = w; this.scratch.height = h;
263	    }
264	    const sc = this.scratch.getContext('2d')!;
265	    sc.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
266	    sc.drawImage(src as CanvasImageSource, x, y, w, h, 0, 0, w, h);
267	    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, e.tex);
268	    gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_PREMULTIPLY_ALPHA_WEBGL, true);   // 同 texFromImage:预乘+mip 能量正确
269	    gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL, false);
270	    gl.texSubImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, x, y, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, this.scratch);
271	    if (e.mipped) gl.generateMipmap(gl.TEXTURE_2D);   // 子区更新后 mip 链重建
272	    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, null);
273	    e.stamp = ++this.stamp;
274	  }
275	
276	  /** ImageData 源直接子区上传(2026-08-18 小地图 CPU 化:主数据已不在画布上,
277	   *  ImageData 不能 drawImage 进 scratch——走 8 参 DOM 源重载直传) */
278	  texSubUpdateData(key: string, src: ImageData, x: number, y: number): void {
279	    if (this.unavailable || !this.gl) return;
280	    const e = this.texs.get(key);
281	    if (!e) return;
282	    const gl = this.gl;
283	    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, e.tex);
284	    gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_PREMULTIPLY_ALPHA_WEBGL, true);
285	    gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL, false);
286	    gl.texSubImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, x, y, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, src);
287	    if (e.mipped) gl.generateMipmap(gl.TEXTURE_2D);
288	    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, null);
289	    e.stamp = ++this.stamp;
290	  }
291	
292	  /** 按键主动释放(全屏地图关闭时丢弃整幅纹理——地图只在打开期占 GPU) */
293	  dropTexture(key: string): void {
294	    if (!this.gl) return;
295	    const e = this.texs.get(key);
296	    if (!e) return;
297	    this.texs.delete(key);
298	    this.bytes -= e.bytes;
299	    this.gl.deleteTexture(e.tex);
300	  }
301	
302	  /** SpriteAtlas DrawRect 便捷入口 */
303	  drawRect(rect: DrawRect, dx: number, dy: number, dw: number, dh: number, opts?: QuadOpts): void {
304	    if (this.unavailable) return;
305	    // ★bitmap 无 .src(旧键退化为 'cv' → 同尺寸跨表碰撞隐患);texId 实例自增
306	    const tag = String(texId(rect.img));
307	    const e = this.texFromImage(`r:${rect.img.width}x${rect.img.height}:${tag}:${rect.sx},${rect.sy}`, rect.img as TexImageSource);
308	    if (!e) return;
309	    this.quad(e, rect.sx, rect.sy, rect.sw, rect.sh, dx, dy, dw, dh, opts);
310	  }
311	
312	  /** 纯色矩形(不透明底/黑盒垫底;tint 即颜色) */
313	  fillQuad(x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, r: number, g: number, b: number, a = 1): void {
314	    if (this.unavailable || !this.gl) return;
315	    const gl = this.gl;

320	    void gl;
321	  }
322	
323	  /** 核心:单 quad。uv 半像素内缩(防 CLAMP 边缘渗色) */
```

```
// F11 图片 → 像素方块群导入(自研功能,原版无)。
// 与游戏逻辑的耦合面仅三处:tile 注册(tiles.ts pixel_block)、渲染分支
// (VanillaTiler.drawPixelBlock)、F11 挂钩(Game.openPixelArtImport)。
// 本模块只做三件纯事:降采样成色格、找放置位、写 tile——全部走 TileStore
// 既有入口(setTile),因此可镐挖/进存档/联机上报自动成立,无任何旁路状态。
import { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../data/tiles';

const PIXEL = TILE_BY_KEY['pixel_block'];

export interface ArtGrid { w: number; h: number; rgba: Uint8ClampedArray }

/** 图片 → 像素格:盒式平均降采样(最长边 ≤ maxSide),通道轻度量化(32 级/通道,
 *  压缩色数更接近像素画);alpha < alphaThresh 的格子标记透明(放置时跳过)。 */
export function imageToGrid(img: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement, maxSide = 48, alphaThresh = 40): ArtGrid {
  const iw = 'naturalWidth' in img ? img.width : img.width;
  const ih = 'naturalHeight' in img ? img.height : img.height;
  const scale = Math.min(1, maxSide / Math.max(iw, ih));
  const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(iw * scale));
  const h = Math.max(1, Math.round(ih * scale));
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = w; c.height = h;
  const cx = c.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true })!;
  cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
  cx.drawImage(img as CanvasImageSource, 0, 0, w, h);
  const d = cx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data;
  // 轻度量化:r/g/b → 32 级(×8 取整回填);透明格 alpha 置 0
  for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4) {
    d[i] = Math.round(d[i] / 8) * 8;
    d[i + 1] = Math.round(d[i + 1] / 8) * 8;
    d[i + 2] = Math.round(d[i + 2] / 8) * 8;
    if (d[i + 3] < alphaThresh) d[i + 3] = 0; else d[i + 3] = 255;
  }
  return { w, h, rgba: d };
}

/** 放置位扫描:锚列(anchorX)自 fromY 向下找地表,底边贴地表悬空放置;
 *  若 width×h 矩形在该高度非全空(地面不平/有障碍),整体逐格抬升直到全空
 *  (上限 300 行,防浮空找不满)。找不到返回 null。
 *  返回 {x, y} = 色格左上角的世界格坐标。 */
export function findArtPlacement(
  st: TileStore, anchorX: number, fromY: number, w: number, h: number,
): { x: number; y: number } | null {
  const x = Math.max(2, Math.min(st.w - w - 2, Math.round(anchorX)));
  if (x + w >= st.w - 1) return null;
  // 地表:锚跨中列自 fromY 向下首个实心
  const cxCol = Math.min(st.w - 1, x + (w >> 1));
  let groundY = -1;
  for (let y = Math.max(1, Math.floor(fromY)); y < st.h - 10; y++) {
    if (st.flags[st.idx(cxCol, y)]) { groundY = y; break; }
```

```
I now have a complete picture. Let me compile the final report. I should organize it by the 6 questions asked, with file paths, line numbers, and code snippets.

Key findings:

## 1. 贴图加载链路

**Entry**: `main.ts:120-141` `loadAssets()` → `atlas = new SpriteAtlas(); await atlas.load()` → hung on `window.__swAtlas`.

**SpriteAtlas.ts** (src/assets/SpriteAtlas.ts) — the single registry. 5 stores:
- `images = new Map<string, ...>` (line 162) — Maples Unity 素材 (atlas.json), key = file path string, value = hardAlpha() canvas
- `vimages = new Map<string, ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement>` (line 169) — 原版 PNG, key = e.g. 'vanilla/Tiles_1.png'
- `uiimages = new Map<string, ...>` (line 171) — vanilla-ui/ UI 贴图, key = file path
- `lazyNpcMeta` (line 252)
- json metadata: `vanilla` (line 161) from public/sprites/vanilla.json — tiles Record<sheetId: string, VanillaTileMeta>, items Record<itemId, VanillaItemMeta>, walls, npcs

**Load chain**: `new Image()` → `im.onload` → `tryBitmapUpgrade()` → `createImageBitmap(img)` → `.then(b => land(b))` → `this.vimages.set(file, store)` → `onVImageLoaded?.(file)` + `bakeTracker.onLoaded(file)`. (SpriteAtlas.ts:432-462 ensureVImage; 363-386 preloadFiles).

`SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP` static flag (line 167-168): `createImageBitmap` available and not `?bitmap=0`.

Draw addressing: `vframe(sheetId, col, row)` (line 219-226) → DrawRect `{img, sx: col*stride[0], sy: row*stride[1], sw: grid[0], sh: grid[1]}`. So a "frame" is not a separate bitmap — it's a sub-rect of the whole sheet bitmap.

Items: shelf-packed at build time into Item_Atlas_0.png / Item_Atlas_1.png (scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs:232-284), meta carries ix/iy/iw/ih sub-rect; `vicon(itemId)` line 345-354.

## 2. 替换点

**The definitive answer**: yes, swapping the Map entry works globally. Evidence:
- All drawing goes through `atlas.ensureVImage(file)` / `vimages.get(...)` per frame (vmisc line 292-299: "命中直接返回(node 测试环境的已注入项同样有效)" — tests inject entries directly into the Map!)
- Comment at line 293: "命中直接返回(node 测试环境的已注入项同样有效)" — the Map is THE injection point already used by tests.

But there are caches that hold derived copies keyed by texture identity (`texId` WeakMap):
- `render/texId.ts:6-12` — WeakMap<object, number> identity
- `SkyRenderer.ts:1451-1513` cloudTintCache (LRU 24 + free pool 8) keyed `${texId(tex)}|${r},${g},${b}` (line 1470-1478; key usage line 770 for flare)
- `TileFlames.ts:376-417` _tintCache keyed `${imgId},${sx},${sy},${sw},${sh},${r&~7},${g&~7},${b&~7}` — replaces the source texture per cell with tinted canvas — this is the closest precedent of "替换源贴图" (draw-time source substitution): at line 403-409 it draws the sheet region into a fresh canvas and multiplies.
- `TintAtlas.ts` (bake/drop) — shared-page tint variant atlas with explicit `drop(key)` for "贴图重载" (line 55-61: "/** 显式丢弃(如贴图重载);不存在则无操作 */")
- `GLSpriteLayer.ts:70,218-247,302-310` — WebGL texture cache `texs = Map<string, TexEntry>` keyed `r:{w}x{h}:{texId}:{sx},{sy}`; has `dropTexture(key)` (line 293) and `texSubUpdate` (line 255). If a bitmap is swapped, the new object gets a new texId → new GL texture; old entry ages out via byte-budget LRU. Consumers: BiomeBackground (#1) and full-screen map (#2).
- BestiaryPanel.ts:568-595 `bstSheetCache` — independent loader precedent (does its own load with cb, but checks `atlas?.vimages.get(file)` first).
- VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts:455,666,679 texCache/slopeTexCache Map<number,...> keyed by liquid style.

Critical: **chunk baked pixels** — ChunkCache bakes tile pixels into canvas pages. Replacing a sheet bitmap does NOT repaint already-baked chunks. The existing self-healing chain to trigger rebake: `onVImageLoaded` → Game.ts:2807-2845 filter (Tiles_/Wall_/CageTop_/Glow_316·317/Tree_*/Shroom/Cactus prefixes) → `chunks.onBakeAssetArrived(file)` → ChunkCache.ts:227-246 debounce 500ms → only re-bakes chunks whose `chunkSheets` set contains that file (registered during bake via bakeTracker.note, ChunkCache.ts:199-211, 439).

Note `invalidateAll()` (ChunkCache.ts:289) exists but is documented as dangerous (384 chunk rebake storm → 21w decodes crash).

**Per-frame active draws** (NPCs, projectiles, liquids, wings, emotes, UI icons, tree tops via WindSway) read the Map every frame → swapping the Map entry is enough, takes effect next frame.

**UI DOM icons**: UI.ts:190-198 paints DrawRect to canvas → `toDataURL()` → img.src. Repaint needed; precedent: `iconUiDirty` flag (Game.ts:2839-2844) with 500ms throttle.

## 3. chunk atlas 页化

ChunkCache.ts:
- Pages: `wallPages`/`tilePages: HTMLCanvasElement[]` 1024² (PAGE_COLS=4, S=CHUNK*TILE*4=1024), CELLS_PER_PAGE=16 (lines 96-116)
- Each chunk = a 256² cell in a page; ChunkPair {wall, tile, sx, sy, cell} (lines 24-32)
- Render: Renderer.ts:2062-2083 drawChunkGrid — `ctx.drawImage(p[layer], p.sx, p.sy, CHUNK*ts, CHUNK*ts, ...)` — draws from the atlas page with source rect.
- Rebake = `markDirty` (252-259) → releasePair → rebuild in flushDirty (4/frame, 6ms budget, line 300-318).
- **Single frame (single tile appearance) replacement**: the tile sheets are NOT atlas-packed at runtime — each `vanilla/Tiles_N.png` is a standalone ImageBitmap; vframe computes sx/sy from stride. So "replace one frame" = either (a) replace the whole sheet bitmap with a canvas that has that one frame region redrawn (e.g., make an HTMLCanvasElement copy of the sheet, drawImage new frame into sx/sy region, set into vimages — canvas is a valid DrawRect.img), or (b) intercept vframe. Option (a) fits the existing architecture perfectly since `images`/`vimages` accept HTMLCanvasElement (DrawRect.img type includes HTMLCanvasElement, line 43).
- Then trigger rebake via `chunks.onBakeAssetArrived(sheetFile)` — but only chunks registered as consuming that sheet rebake. Or markDirtyArea for a region. Full: invalidateAll.

## 4. 存档/持久化先例

- `mainFlow.ts:842-861 doExportSave()` — Blob + URL.createObjectURL + a.download → `.sbw.json` download. THE export precedent.
- `main.ts:308-315 downloadText()` — generic text downloader.
- `DebugReport.ts:888-891`, `UI.ts:557-560` same pattern.
- IndexedDB: `save/KvStore.ts` — kvSet/kvGet/kvGetIdb (kv store 'kv' in DB 'sandboxworld'), 2MB threshold to localStorage (lines 60-80). WorldStore/CharacterStore in save/ folder use it.
- localStorage keys: sbw.achievements.v1, sbw.research.v1, swAssetsComplete:<version> (AssetCache.ts:176).
- Save workers: save.worker.ts (serialize off-main-thread), worldGen.worker.ts (gen + saveParse + previewBitmap RGBA).
- Binary file (image) storage: **no precedent found** for storing blobs/binary in IndexedDB (KvStore only stores strings). A texture pack would need either string (dataURL) or a new blob-capable store. F11 PixelArtImport (src/dev/PixelArtImport.ts) imports images but doesn't persist them as textures — it converts to tiles.

## 5. 素材文件本体

- `public/sprites/vanilla/` — 9195 PNGs (764 Tiles_*, 840 NPC_*, 367 Wall_*, Item_Atlas_0/1, misc Projectile_/Glow_/Cloud_ etc.) — extracted from `../terraria-assets/Images` by `scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs` (whitelist `scripts/vanilla-whitelist.json`, metadata from `../Terraria-Map-Editor` TEdit data json).
- `public/sprites/vanilla-ui/` — 1779 PNGs.
- `public/sprites/地形` (10), `角色` (6), `portraits` (28), `封面` (4) — Maples Unity assets.
- Manifests: `public/sprites/vanilla.json` (1.35MB: tiles 753 / items 6119 / walls 366 / npcs / armorIndex / names), `vanilla-ui.json` (1509 file keys), `vanilla-npcs.json`, `vanilla-npcgore.json`, `atlas.json`, `resources.json`, `annotations.json`.
- Total public/: 12014 files, 11022 png, 852 wav, 106 mp3.
- No existing "素材包/资源包" directory concept. Closest: `public/sw.js` + `src/net/AssetCache.ts` Service Worker Cache API asset pipeline (version = fnv1a32(vanilla.json+vanilla-ui.json+CACHE_BUSTER)); it intercepts /sprites|fonts|l10n|sounds|audios GETs, cache-first. **A hot-patch could either go through the SW (change what network/cache serves) or bypass it by directly swapping Map entries in memory.**

## 6. Worker/主线程

- Texture decode is **main thread only**: `new Image()` + `createImageBitmap` inside SpriteAtlas. No OffscreenCanvas, no ImageBitmap transfer.
- Workers: worldGen.worker.ts (world gen + save parse + previewBitmap RGBA generation — pure typed arrays, no DOM), save.worker.ts (serialize). previewBitmap.ts is executed in the worldgen worker for the world creation preview (renderer-side preview receives RGBA and putImageData on main thread).
- So a texture hot-patch has **no thread boundary issues** — everything is main thread. ImageBitmap created on main thread is directly usable. The only caveat: createImageBitmap failure under GPU pressure is a known issue (tryBitmapUpgrade with 10/20/40s backoff retry, bmpFailStats, SpriteAtlas.ts:1025-1070), so hot-patch loading should use the same bridge or accept HTMLImageElement fallback.
- ImageBitmap is transferable/serializable but nothing here needs that.

Also worth mentioning:
- `hardAlpha()` (SpriteAtlas.ts:141-156) — Maples images get hard-alpha canvas treatment; vanilla vimages get "干净像素,不做 hardAlpha". A replacement bitmap should match raw-pixel semantics for vanilla.
- `SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP` gate and `?bitmap=0` escape hatch.
- `BestiaryPanel.bstLoadSheet` — independent loader with its own cache, checks atlas.vimages first.
- The annotator.html / sheet-probe.html / tree-inspect.html / hitbox-lab.html debug tools in public/.
- dist/ is a build output (vite build copies public/ + assets).
- vanilla.json items: only 2 icon files (Item_Atlas_0.png, Item_Atlas_1.png) for 6119 items — build-time shelf-pack.

Now let me write the final report. I should be comprehensive but organized. The user asked for specific paths + line numbers + code snippets.
```

````
调研已完成。以下是完整报告。

# SandboxWorld 贴图系统调研报告(素材包热补丁规划用)

## 1. 贴图加载链路:文件 → ImageBitmap → 注册表

**注册表是单一类**:`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/assets/SpriteAtlas.ts`(全项目唯一贴图注册表,`main.ts:121` 实例化后挂 `window.__swAtlas`)。

四个存储 Map,全部**以文件路径字符串为 key**(不是数字 id):

```ts
// SpriteAtlas.ts:158-172
export class SpriteAtlas {
  images   = new Map<string, ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement>();  // Maples Unity 素材(hardAlpha 后的 canvas)
  vimages  = new Map<string, ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement>(); // ★原版素材,key 如 'vanilla/Tiles_1.png'
  uiimages = new Map<string, ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement>(); // UI 贴图,key 如 'vanilla-ui/logo_1.png'
  vanilla  = vanillaJson as unknown as VanillaData;             // 元数据(public/sprites/vanilla.json)
```

**加载链路**(vimages 主通道,`SpriteAtlas.ts:432-462 ensureVImage`,懒加载核心):

```
new Image() → im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`  (相对路径,public/ 下)
  → im.onload
    → tryBitmapUpgrade(im, ...)                       (SpriteAtlas.ts:1049-1070)
      → createImageBitmap(img).then(b => land(b))     // 主线程解码,USE_BITMAP 门(167-168 行,?bitmap=0 逃生)
    → land(store): this.vimages.set(file, store)      // ★写入点
      → bakeTracker.onLoaded(file) + onVImageLoaded?.(file)  // 晚到钩子(见 §3)
```

失败有 10/20/40s 退避重试(`BMP_RETRY_DELAYS`,1034 行),重试成功会**二次 land 原地替换 Map 项**——这就是"运行时换源"的既有先例。

**索引方式**:元数据 `vanilla.json` 中 `tiles: Record<sheetId, VanillaTileMeta>`,`meta.sheet = 'vanilla/Tiles_1.png'`、`grid:[16,16]`、`stride:[18,18]`。取帧(`SpriteAtlas.ts:219-226`):

```ts
vframe(sheetId, col, row): DrawRect | null {
  const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);   // 每次取都走 Map
  return { img, sx: col * m.stride[0], sy: row * m.stride[1], sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
}
```

**关键结论:一"帧"不是独立 bitmap,是整张 sheet bitmap 的子矩形**。`DrawRect.img` 类型为 `ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement`(43 行)——canvas 也是合法源。物品图标在构建期已 shelf-pack 进 2 张 `Item_Atlas_0/1.png`(6059 单体→2 张,`scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs:232-284`),`vicon()`(345-354 行)按 `ix/iy/iw/ih` 子矩形取。

## 2. 替换点:换 Map 项即可全局生效,但有四类派生缓存

**最可行注入点 = `atlas.vimages.set(file, newSource)`**。理由:
- 所有消费方每帧经 `ensureVImage`/`vmisc`/`vui`/`vframe` 现查 Map(注释 `SpriteAtlas.ts:293` 明说"命中直接返回(node 测试环境的已注入项同样有效)"——**测试已经在用直接注入 Map 的方式替换贴图**);
- NPC/弹幕/液体/翅膀/UI 图标等"每帧活画"路径,下一帧即生效;
- `ImageBitmap` 不可变,所以替换 = 换对象引用,没有任何代码持有旧 bitmap 的长期引用(除了下列缓存)。

**需要同步失效的派生缓存家族**(全部按"贴图身份" WeakMap 发号,`src/render/texId.ts:6-12`):

| 缓存 | 位置 | 键 | 失效手段 |
|---|---|---|---|
| cloudTintCache | `SkyRenderer.ts:1451-1513` | `${texId(tex)}\|${r},${g},${b}` | 换新对象后 texId 自变,旧项 LRU(24) 自然淘汰 |
| _tintCache(火焰) | `TileFlames.ts:376-417` | `${imgId},${sx},${sy},...` | 同上,上限 512 |
| TintAtlas | `TintAtlas.ts`(染色变体页化图集) | 调用方字符串 | **已有 `drop(key)`(55-61 行),注释即"显式丢弃(如贴图重载)"** |
| GLSpriteLayer.texs | `GLSpriteLayer.ts:70,218-247` | `r:{w}x{h}:{texId}:{sx},{sy}` | 换对象→新 texId→新纹理;旧项按字节预算 LRU;有 `dropTexture(key)`(293 行)与 `texSubUpdate`(255 行) |
| 液体 texCache | `VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts:455,666,679` | style 数字 | 需手动清 |
| BestiaryPanel 独立缓存 | `BestiaryPanel.ts:568-595` | 文件名 | 先查 `atlas.vimages`,注入后新开面板即新 |

**做过"替换源贴图"类似事情的先例**:`TileFlames.ts:381-417 tintedFlameCell`——draw 时把 sheet 的某帧区域**拷进新 canvas 做 multiply 染色再作为源**(403-409 行),即"运行时合成替代源";`TintAtlas.drop` 的注释直接预告了贴图重载场景。另外 `tryBitmapUpgrade` 的"重试成功 onReady(bitmap) 把持有方的 Image 原地换掉"(1045-1048 行注释)就是一次已落地的全局换源。

**另一个必须处理的点——chunk 烘焙像素**:见 §3。

## 3. chunk atlas 页化(ChunkCache)

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/ChunkCache.ts`:

- **页结构**(96-163 行):墙/tile 各一摞 1024² canvas 页(`wallPages`/`tilePages`),每页 4×4 = 16 个 256² cell(`CELLS_PER_PAGE=16`, `PAGE_COLS=4`)。chunk 16×16 tile = 一个 cell。`ChunkPair { wall, tile, sx, sy, cell }`(24-32 行)。
- **渲染**(Renderer.ts:2062-2083):`ctx.drawImage(p[layer], p.sx, p.sy, CHUNK*ts, CHUNK*ts, ...)` 从页画布取源矩形。
- **烘焙**(416-445 `bakeChunkInto`):每 cell clip+translate 原位重画,调 `drawVanillaCell`(VanillaTiler.ts:869)等,源就是 `atlas.vimages` 里的 sheet。

**替换单帧(单个物块外观)是否支持?支持,且无需动 atlas 机制**:
- tile sheet 运行时**不是**合并 atlas,每张 `vanilla/Tiles_N.png` 是独立 bitmap,帧=sx/sy 子矩形;
- 所以"只替换某帧"最自然的做法:**做一张 sheet 尺寸的 HTMLCanvasElement,先 drawImage 原表,再把新帧画到 (sx,sy) 区域,然后 `vimages.set('vanilla/Tiles_N.png', canvas)`**——canvas 是合法 `DrawRect.img`,所有 vframe/vframeAt/vrect/vmisc 路径零改动;
- 然后**触发重烘**。现成的精确打击链路(这就是"晚到贴图重烘"同款):
  - `Game.ts:2807-2845`:`atlas.onVImageLoaded = (file) => { if (file.startsWith('vanilla/Tiles_')...) this.chunks.onBakeAssetArrived(file) }`
  - `ChunkCache.ts:227-246 onBakeAssetArrived`:500ms 去抖合批,只重烘 `chunkSheets`(烘焙期经 `bakeTracker.note` 登记,199-211 行)含该文件的 chunk;
  - 热补丁可直接调 `chunks.onBakeAssetArrived(sheetFile)` 复用整条链,或 `markDirtyArea(x0,y0,x1,y1)`(268 行)指定区域;
  - ⚠️ `invalidateAll()`(289 行)存在但被注释多次警告为危险路径(384 chunk 重烘风暴→21 万次重解码→崩溃,`ChunkCache.ts:284-285`、`Game.ts:2803-2805`)。
- 已烘焙动画 chunk(animChunksBySheet,83 行)会按 sheet 换带自动重烘,替换 sheet 后同样吃到该机制。

## 4. 存档/持久化先例

**导出下载先例(可直接抄)**:
- `src/mainFlow.ts:842-861 doExportSave()`:`new Blob([json], {type:'application/json'})` → `URL.createObjectURL` → `a.download = filename; a.click()` → 10s 后 revoke。文件名 `${worldName}-${date}.sbw.json`。
- `src/main.ts:308-315 downloadText()` 通用文本下载;`DebugReport.ts:888-891`、`UI.ts:557-560` 同款。

**IndexedDB/localStorage 先例**:
- `src/save/KvStore.ts` — DB `sandboxworld`、store `kv`;`kvSet`(61-80 行)字符串 ≤2MB 进 localStorage 否则只进 IDB;`kvGetIdb`(38-50 行)是 worker 安全只读版。**只存字符串,没有 blob/二进制存储先例**(素材包若存 IndexedDB 需新增 blob 支持,或走 dataURL 字符串)。
- localStorage 键先例:`sbw.achievements.v1`(mainFlow.ts:70-71)、`sbw.research.v1`(Research.ts:51-54)、`swAssetsComplete:<version>`(AssetCache.ts:176-189)。
- **用户图片导入先例**:`src/dev/PixelArtImport.ts`(F1/F11 图片→像素方块群)——有图片解码→canvas→getImageData 全链,但不持久化为贴图。

## 5. 素材文件本体

`public/` 共 12014 文件(11022 png / 852 wav / 106 mp3):

| 目录 | 文件数 | 内容 |
|---|---|---|
| `public/sprites/vanilla/` | 9195 png | 原版素材:Tiles_* 764 张、NPC_* 840、Wall_* 367、Item_Atlas_0/1、Glow_/Projectile_/Cloud_/Tree_Tops_32 张等 |
| `public/sprites/vanilla-ui/` | 1779 png | UI 贴图(vanilla-ui.json 1509 键) |
| `public/sprites/地形|角色|封面|portraits` | 10/6/4/28 | Maples Unity 素材 |
| `public/sprites/*.json` | 7 | vanilla.json(1.35MB:tiles 753/items 6119/walls 366/npcs/armorIndex)、vanilla-ui.json、vanilla-npcs.json、atlas.json、resources.json、annotations.json |

- 生成链:`scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs`——从 `../terraria-assets/Images` 按白名单(`scripts/vanilla-whitelist.json`)拷 PNG + 从 `../Terraria-Map-Editor` TEdit 数据提取网格元数据;`scripts/build-atlas.mjs` 生成 Maples 侧。
- **没有现成"素材包/资源包"目录概念**。最接近的基建是 **Service Worker 资产管线**:`public/sw.js` + `src/net/AssetCache.ts`——SW 拦截 `/sprites|fonts|l10n|sounds|audios` 的 GET 走 Cache API(sw.js:13, 37-97),版本 = `fnv1a32(vanilla.json + vanilla-ui.json + CACHE_BUSTER)`(AssetCache.ts:33-48)。热补丁有两条路:**(a) 走 SW/Cache API 层拦截 URL**(对 `new Image()` 透明,零代码改动,但缓存版本号机制会干扰);**(b) 内存层直接换 `vimages` Map 项**(本报告 §2,推荐,已有测试注入先例)。
- `dist/` 是 vite 构建产物(public/ 的拷贝 + assets),非运行时素材源。

## 6. Worker/主线程边界

**贴图解码全部在主线程**,无任何 worker/offscreen 参与:
- `new Image()` + `createImageBitmap` 都在 SpriteAtlas(主线程)内;无 OffscreenCanvas、无 ImageBitmap transfer;
- 三个 worker 均不碰贴图:`worldGen.worker.ts`(世界生成/存档解析/预览位图 RGBA——`previewBitmap.ts` 是纯 typed array 着色,主线程 putImageData)、`save.worker.ts`(RLE+base64+JSON 序列化)、`SaveClient.ts`(worker 门面,失败回退主线程 `saveGame`,`SaveFile.ts:39`);
- 记忆中的"worker packet" = `src/workers/worldPacket.ts`(世界数据 Arraybuffer 转移包,与贴图无关)。

**结论:热补丁无线程边界问题**,全部逻辑可留主线程。唯一注意事项:
1. `createImageBitmap` 在 GPU 压力窗口期会失败(项目已知问题,`SpriteAtlas.ts:1025-1044 bmpFailStats`),热补丁加载应复用 `tryBitmapUpgrade`/`loadBitmapOnly`(1076-1089 行)带 Image 回退+退避重试的桥;
2. `SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP` 静态门与 `?bitmap=0` 逃生参数需尊重;
3. Maples 侧 `images` 有 `hardAlpha()` 处理(141-156 行),vanilla 侧 vimages 是"干净像素不做 hardAlpha"(169 行注释)——替换贴图应按 vanilla 语义直接入表,不做 alpha 二值化。

## 推荐注入点总结

1. **加载**:热补丁文件(File/dataURL)→ `createImageBitmap` → 走 `tryBitmapUpgrade` 同款失败回退;
2. **注入**:`atlas.vimages.set('vanilla/Tiles_N.png', source)`(或合成 canvas 替换单帧);`uiimages` 同理;
3. **失效**:`TintAtlas.drop` / GL 层换对象自动新 texId / UI 图标走 `iconUiDirty` 节流刷新(Game.ts:2839-2844);
4. **重烘**:tile 类调 `game.chunks.onBakeAssetArrived(file)` 复用精确重烘链(500ms 去抖、只烘消费过该 sheet 的 chunk);严禁盲目 `invalidateAll`;
5. **持久化**:素材包 manifest+图可选 KvStore 扩展(现只存字符串)或导出下载(`doExportSave` 模式)。
````
